स मामुपगतः क्रुद्धो वज्रमुद्यम्य देवराट् ।  
ततोऽहं सहसा क्षिप्तः श्वसनेन महात्मना ॥ १२५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स मामुपगतः क्रुद्धो वज्रमुद्यम्य देवराट् ।  
ततोऽहं सहसा क्षिप्तः श्वसनेन महात्मना ॥ १२५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

ततः क्रुद्धः सहस्राअक्षः पर्वतानां शतक्रतुः ।
पक्षान् चिच्छेद वज्रेण तत्र तत्र सहस्रशः ॥५-१-१२५
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘उस समय कुपित हुए देवराज इन्द्र वज्र उठाये मेरी ओर भी आये, किन्तु महात्मा वायुने सहसा मुझे इस समुद्रमें गिरा दिया ॥ १२५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

क्रुद्धनाद देवेन्द्रनु वज्रायुधवन्नॆत्तिकॊण्डु नन्न रॆक्कॆगळन्नु कत्तरिसलु बळिगॆ बन्दनु. आ समयक्कॆ सरियागि महात्मनाद निन्न तन्दॆ वायुदेवरु नन्नन्नु हारिसिकॊण्डु होगि समुद्रदल्लि इरिसिदनु. ॥125 ॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*125. tataH* = after that; *kruddhaH* = angry;
*sahasraakshaH* = Devendra; *shata krataH* = with a hundred yajnas;
*chichheda* = cut off; *pakshaan* = wings; *sahasrashaH parvataanaam* = of
thousands of mountains; *vajreNa* = by his weapon vajra; *tatra tatra* = then and
there.

**अनुवादः**

"Then the angry Devendra who performed a hundred Asvamedha rituals, cut off the
wings of thousands of mountains by his weapon vajra then and there."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘When the wrathful king of the gods (_devarāṭ_) approached me with upraised thunderbolt (_vajram udyamya_), I was at once swept away (_kṣiptaḥ_) by the great-souled Wind-god (_śvasanena_).’
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**125–126 (Vāyu's Rescue of Maināka)**

When the furious king of the gods advanced upon Maināka with raised thunderbolt, the great-souled Wind-god (श्वसनेन वायुना) snatched him up—_Tilaka_ glosses क्षिप्तः as `hurled through the sky' (आकाशे क्षिप्तः) and _Śiromaṇi_ as `whisked out of reach' (अस्पृष्टं प्रापितः)—and plunged him into the salt sea. Both Govindarāja and _Śiromaṇi_ note the reading गुप्तपक्षसमग्रश्च, explaining that Vāyu preserved Maināka whole with his wings safely hidden beneath the waves (समग्रः समग्रपक्षः गुप्तसमग्रपक्षश्च यथा भवामि तथा अभिरक्षितोऽस्मीत्यर्थः).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अस्मिल्ँ लवणतोये च प्रक्षिप्तः प्लवगोत्तम ।  
गुप्तपक्षः समग्रश्च तव पित्राभिरक्षितः ॥ १२६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अस्मिल्ँ लवणतोये च प्रक्षिप्तः प्लवगोत्तम ।  
गुप्तपक्षः समग्रश्च तव पित्राभिरक्षितः ॥ १२६ ॥
_________________
अस्मिन् लवणतोये च प्रक्षिप्तः प्लवगोत्तम ।  
गुप्तपक्षसमग्रश्च तव पित्राभिरक्षितः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

स मामुपागतः क्रुद्धो वज्रमुद्यम्य देवराट् ।
ततोऽहं सहसा क्षिप्तः स्वसनेन महात्मना ॥ ५-१-१२६
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘वानरश्रेष्ठ! इस क्षार समुद्रमें गिराकर आपके पिताने मेरे पंखोंकी रक्षा कर ली और मैं अपने सम्पूर्ण अंशसे सुरक्षित बच गया ॥ १२६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ओ वानरोत्तमा! निन्न तन्दॆयाद वायुदेवरु नन्नन्नु समुद्रदल्लिरिसि, नन्नन्न मात्रवल्लदॆ, नन्न समग्रवाद रॆक्कॆगळन्नु संरक्षिसिदनु. अवनिन्दागि नानु ई लवणसमुद्रदल्लि भद्रवागिद्देनॆ. ॥126॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*126. saH* = that; *devaraaT* = Devendra; *upaagataH* =
reached; *maam* = me; *kruddhaH* = with anger; *udyamya* = raising;
*vajram* = vajra; *tataH* = then; *aham* = I; *kshiptaH* = was thrown
away; *sahasaa* = immediately; *mahaatmanaa shvasanena* = by the great Vayu.

**अनुवादः**

"That Devendra angrily reached me with raised vajra. Then I was thrown away
immediately by the great Vayu."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘And cast down (_prakṣiptaḥ_) into this salt ocean, O best of leapers, with my wings concealed (_guptapakṣaḥ_) and intact (_samagraś ca_), I was preserved (_abhirakṣitaḥ_) by your father!’
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Though Maināka earnestly begs him to rest, Hanūmān declines to tarry because the day is waning and his vow forbids stopping, touching the peak affectionately as he speeds onward.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ततोऽहं मानयामि त्वां मान्योऽसि मम मारुते ।  
त्वया ममैष सम्बन्धः कपिमुख्य महागुणः ॥ १२७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ततोऽहं मानयामि त्वां मान्योऽसि मम मारुते ।  
त्वया ममैष सम्बन्धः कपिमुख्य महागुणः ॥ १२७ ॥
_________________
ततोऽहं मानयामि त्वां मान्यो हि मम मारुतः ।  
त्वया मे ह्येष सम्बन्धः कपिमुख्य महागुणः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘पवननन्दन! कपिश्रेष्ठ! इसीलिये मैं आपका आदर करता हूँ, आप मेरे माननीय हैं । आपके साथ मेरा यह सम्बन्ध महान् गुणोंसे युक्त है ॥ १२७ ॥
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘Therefore I honor you (_mānayāmi tvām_), and you are worthy of my honor, O son of Māruta! This bond of mine with you, O chief of monkeys, bears great virtue (_mahāguṇaḥ_).’
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**127–129 (Maināka's Final Plea)**

Because Vāyu saved his wings, Maināka reveres Vāyu's son (तत उक्तकारणादेष सम्बन्धः पूजाप्रयोजकः). Rāma Varmā's _Tilaka_ explains अस्मिन्नेवंगते कार्ये as `now that this long-awaited opportunity for reciprocation has finally arrived' (चिरप्रवृत्ते सति), urging Hanūmān to gladden both Sāgara and Maināka by accepting their hospitality (पूजास्वीकारेण तयोः प्रीतिं कर्तुमर्हसि).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अस्मिन् नेवङ्गते कार्ये सागरस्य ममैव च ।  
प्रीतिं प्रीतमनाः कर्तुं त्वमर्हसि महामते ॥ १२८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अस्मिन् नेवङ्गते कार्ये सागरस्य ममैव च ।  
प्रीतिं प्रीतमनाः कर्तुं त्वमर्हसि महामते ॥ १२८ ॥
_________________
अस्मिन्नेवं गते कार्ये सागरस्य ममैव च ।  
प्रीतिं प्रीतमनाः कर्तुं त्वमर्हसि महाकपे ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘महामते! इस प्रकार चिरकालके बाद जो यह प्रत्युपकाररूप कार्य (आपके पिताके उपकारका बदला चुकानेका अवसर) प्राप्त हुआ है, इसमें आप प्रसन्नचित्त होकर मेरी और समुद्रकी भी प्रीतिका सम्पादन करें (हमारा आतिथ्य ग्रहण करके हमें संतुष्ट करें) ॥ १२८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ओ कपिमुख्यनाद मारुतिये! निन्नॊडनॆ ननगॆ निन्न तन्दॆय उपकाररूपवाद महागुणयुक्तवाद ई सम्बन्धविदॆ. अदरिन्दले नानु निन्नन्नु सन्मानिसुत्तिद्देनॆ. निन्न तन्दॆयन्तॆ नीनू ननगॆ मान्यनागिरुवॆ. ओ महामतियाद कपीश्वरा! इन्तह सुवर्णावकाशदिन्द नीनु सन्तोष मनस्सिनिन्द आतिथ्यवन्नु स्वीकरिसि ननगू, समुद्रनिगू आनन्दगॊळिसु.॥127-128॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*127. plavagottama* = O best among vanaras! gupta paksha samagrashcha =
with saved full wings; *prakshiptaH* = being thrown down; *asmin lavaNatoye* = in
this salt ocean; *abhirakshitaH* = I have been protected; *tava pitraa* = by your
father.

**अनुवादः**

"O best among vanaras! With saved full wings, being thrown down into this salt
ocean, I have been protected by your father."
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*128. kapi mukhya* = O chief among vanaras! maarutaH = Vayu;
*maanyaH hi* = is indeed respectable; *mama* = to me; *tataH* = for that
reason; *aham* = I; *maanayaami* = am respecting; *tvaam* = you; *me* =
my; *eshhaH* = this; *saMbandhaH* = relationship; *tvayaa* = with you;
*mahaa guNaH* = has great virtues.

**अनुवादः**

"O chief among vanaras! Vayu is thus indeed respectable to me. For that reason I am
respecting you. My this relationship with you has great virtues."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘Since this obligation (_kārye_) of the Ocean and of myself has thus arisen (_evaṃgate_), you ought, with pleased mind (_prītamanāḥ_), to grant us this satisfaction (_prītiṃ kartum_), O great-minded one!’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

श्रमं मोक्षय पूजां च गृहाण हरिसत्तम ।  
प्रीतिं च मम मान्यस्य प्रीतोऽस्मि तव दर्शनात् ॥ १२९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

श्रमं मोक्षय पूजां च गृहाण हरिसत्तम ।  
प्रीतिं च मम मान्यस्य प्रीतोऽस्मि तव दर्शनात् ॥ १२९ ॥
_________________
श्रमं मोक्षय पूजां च गृहाण कपिसत्तम ।  
प्रीतिं च बहु मन्यस्व प्रीतोऽस्मि तव दर्शनात् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘वानरशिरोमणे! आप यहाँ अपनी थकान उतारिये, हमारी पूजा ग्रहण कीजिये और मेरे प्रेमको भी स्वीकार कीजिये । मैं आप-जैसे माननीय पुरुषके दर्शनसे बहुत प्रसन्न हुआ हूँ’ ॥ १२९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ओ कपिसत्तमा! निन्न दर्शनदिन्दले नानु परम प्रीतनागिद्देनॆ. इल्लि स्वल्प विश्रमिसिकॊण्डु श्रमवन्नु परिहरिसिकॊ; नम्म सत्कारवन्नु स्वीकरिसु. निन्न मेलॆ ननगुण्टागिरुव विश्वासवन्नु पुरस्करिसु.’’ ॥129॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*129. mahaa kape* = O great vanara ! asmin kaarye = (because) this
matter; *evaMgate* = goes like this; *tvam* = you; *arhasi* = are suitable;
*kartum* = to do; *priitim* = happiness; *saagarasya* = to Sagara; *mamaiva
cha* = and to me; *priitamanaaH* = with a gladdened heart.

**अनुवादः**

"O great Vanara! Because this matter is like this, you are suitable to make myself
and Sagara happy, with a gladdened heart."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘Relieve your fatigue (_śramaṃ mokṣaya_) and accept my worship (_pūjāṃ ca gṛhāṇa_), O foremost of monkeys, as well as the affection of me who hold you in honor—for I am delighted by the sight of you (_tava darśanāt_)!’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एवमुक्तः कपिश्रेष्ठस्तं नगोत्तममब्रवीत् ।  
प्रीतोऽस्मि कृतमातिथ्यं मन्युरेषोऽपनीयताम् ॥ १३० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एवमुक्तः कपिश्रेष्ठस्तं नगोत्तममब्रवीत् ।  
प्रीतोऽस्मि कृतमातिथ्यं मन्युरेषोऽपनीयताम् ॥ १३० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

मैनाकके ऐसा कहनेपर कपिश्रेष्ठ हनुमान् जी ने उस उत्तम पर्वतसे कहा—‘मैनाक! मुझे भी आपसे मिलकर बड़ी प्रसन्नता हुई है । मेरा आतिथ्य हो गया । अब आप अपने मनसे यह दुःख अथवा चिन्ता निकाल दीजिये कि इन्होंने मेरी पूजा ग्रहण नहीं की ॥ १३० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

हीगॆ मैनाकनु विनीतनागि हेळलु कपिश्रेष्ठनाद हनुमन्तनु यथोचितवागि हीगॆ नुडिदनु ‘‘ऎलै मैनाकने! निन्न मातुगळिन्दले नानु सुप्रीतनागिद्देनॆ. निन्न ई सविनुडिगळिन्द नीनु आतिथ्यवन्नु माडिदन्तॆ आगिदॆ. ‘इष्टु प्रार्थिसिकॊण्डरू आतिथ्यवन्नु स्वीकरिसलिल्लवल्ल’ ऎम्ब बेसरवन्नु बिट्टुबिडु. अन्यथा भाविसबेड.॥130॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*130. kapisattama* = O best among Vanaras! mokshaya = reduce;
*shramam* = tiredness; *gR^ihaaNa* = accept; *puujaam cha* = (our) worship
also; *bahumanyasva* = respect; *priitim* = (our) love; *priitaH asmi* = I am
happy; *tava darshanaat* = by your appearance.

**अनुवादः**

"O best among Vanaras! Reduce your fatigue. Accept our worship also. Respect our
love. I am happy by your manifestation."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Thus addressed, the best of monkeys spoke to that foremost of mountains: ‘I am pleased (_prīto &#x27;smi_), hospitality has already been rendered (_kṛtam ātithyam_); let this distress (_manyuḥ_) be set aside!’
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**130–131 (Gracious Refusal and the Twofold Urgency)**

Hanūmān replies with consummate diplomacy: `I am pleased; hospitality is already accomplished (कृतमातिथ्यम्) simply by your affectionate words and sight (तव दर्शनादिनेति भावः)!' Commenting on मन्युरेषोऽपनीयताम्, _Tilaka_ and _Śiromaṇi_ gloss मन्युः as `dejection' (पूजानङ्गीकारजं दैन्यम्) over his not halting, whereas Govindarāja glosses it as `anger' (मत्कृता पूजा न गृहीतेति कोपो निरस्यताम्). Hanūmān gives three reasons for not stopping: his mission's deadline presses (कार्यकालः त्वरते), daylight is waning—proving, as _Śiromaṇi_ notes, that he must reach Laṅkā within the single day (एतेन दिन एव लङ्का गन्तव्येति सूचितम्)—and he pledged before the monkeys not to pause mid-ocean (समुद्रे अन्तरे मध्ये मया न स्थातव्यमिति वानरसन्निधौ प्रतिज्ञा कृता).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त्वरते कार्यकालो मे अहश्चाप्यतिवर्तते ।  
प्रतिज्ञा च मया दत्ता न स्थातव्यमिहान्तरा ॥ १३१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त्वरते कार्यकालो मे अहश्चाप्यतिवर्तते ।  
प्रतिज्ञा च मया दत्ता न स्थातव्यमिहान्तरा ॥ १३१ ॥
_________________
त्वरते कार्यकालो मे ह्यहश्च व्यतिवर्तते ।  
प्रतिज्ञा च मया दत्ता न स्थातव्यमिहान्तरे ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘मेरे कार्यका समय मुझे बहुत जल्दी करनेके लिये प्रेरित कर रहा है । यह दिन भी बीता जा रहा है । मैंने वानरोंके समीप यह प्रतिज्ञा कर ली है कि मैं यहाँ बीचमें कहीं नहीं ठहर सकता’ ॥ १३१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

कार्यद समयवु नन्नन्नु आतुरगॊळिसुत्तदॆ. हगलु कळॆदुहोगुत्तिदॆ. ‘मार्गमध्यदल्लि ऎल्लियू निल्लुवुदिल्ल’ ऎन्दु मित्रर मुन्दॆ प्रतिज्ञॆ माडिरुवॆनु.’’ ॥131॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*131. kapi shhreshhThaH* = Hanuma; *evam uktaH* = thus being
talked to; *abraviit* = spoke (thus); *taM nagottamam* = to that best among
mountains; *priitaH asmi* = I am happy; *aatithyam* = hospitality; *kR^itam* =
has been done; *esshaH manyuH* = (let) this anger (or ill feeling); *apaniiyataam* =
be removed.

**अनुवादः**

Hanuma listening to the best mountain's words, spoke thus : "I am happy. Hospitality
has been done. Let this ill feeling be removed."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘The time for my mission presses (_tvarate kāryakālo me_) and the day is passing away (_ativartate_); moreover, a vow (_pratijñā_) has been given by me not to halt midway (_antarā_) here!’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

इत्युक्त्वा पाणिना शैलमालभ्य हरिपुङ्गवः ।  
जगामाकाशमाविश्य वीर्यवान् प्रहसन्निव ॥ १३२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

इत्युक्त्वा पाणिना शैलमालभ्य हरिपुङ्गवः ।  
जगामाकाशमाविश्य वीर्यवान् प्रहसन्निव ॥ १३२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

त्वरते कार्यकालो मे अहश्चाप्यतिवर्तते ।
प्रतिज्ञा च मया दत्ता न स्थातव्यमिहान्त्तरे ॥ ५-१-१३२
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

ऐसा कहकर महाबली वानरशिरोमणि हनुमान् ने हँसते हुएसे वहाँ मैनाकका अपने हाथसे स्पर्श किया और आकाशमें ऊपर उठकर चलने लगे ॥ १३२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

कपिश्रेष्ठनाद, वीर्यवन्तनाद हनुमन्तनु मैनाकनिगॆ हीगॆ समाधान हेळि, अवन मै दडवि अवन आतिथ्यवन्नु स्वीकरिसि नगु-नगुत्तले आकाशमार्गवागि मुन्दक्कॆ हॊरटनु.॥132॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*132. kaalaH* = time; *me kaaryaH* = (to perform) my duty;
*tvarate* = is hastening (me); *ahaH cha* = the day too; *ativartate* = is
passing; *pratijJNaa* = a promise; *dattaa cha* = has also been given; *mayaa*
= by me; *na sthaatavyam* = (I) should not stand; *antare* = in the middle;
*iha* = here (neglecting my duty).

**अनुवादः**

"The time to perform my duty is hastening me. The day too is passing. A promise has
also been given by me. I should not stand here in the middle neglecting my duty."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Having spoken thus, the heroic bull among monkeys touched (_ālabhya_) the mountain with his hand (_pāṇinā_) and, entering the sky, sped on as though smiling (_prahasann iva_).
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**132 (The Touch of Grace and Smiling Departure)**

Touching the peak with his hand (शैलमालभ्य स्पृष्ट्वा), Hanūmān bounds back into the sky `as if smiling' (प्रहसन्निव). While Govindarāja takes प्रहसन्निव as `with a cheerful face' (प्रसन्नवदनः), _Tilaka_ discerns a gentle, heroic amusement: how quaint that Maināka and Sāgara imagine so brief a leap could tire him (एतावत्प्लवनेऽपि श्रममारोप्य वृथाभ्यां प्रयास एतयोरिति प्रहासः)!
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Blessed by the mountain and the sea, Hanūmān soars higher along his father's aerial path while the gods and Thousand-Eyed Indra himself praise golden Maināka.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स पर्वतसमुद्राभ्यां बहुमानादवेक्षितः ।  
पूजितश्चोपपन्नाभिराशीर्भिरभिनन्दितः ॥ १३३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

_________________
स पर्वतसमुद्राभ्यां बहुमानादवेक्षितः ।  
पूजितश्चोपपन्नाभिराशीर्भिरनिलात्मजः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

उस समय पर्वत और समुद्र दोनोंने ही बड़े आदरसे उनकी ओर देखा, उनका सत्कार किया और यथोचित आशीर्वादोंसे उनका अभिनन्दन किया ॥ १३३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आग समुद्रराजनू, मैनाकनू वायुसूनु मारुतियन्नु हॆच्चिन आदर अभिमानदिन्द वीक्षिसुत्तिद्दरु. भक्तितत्पररागि पूजिसिदरु. रामकार्य सिद्धिगागि कालोचितवाद आशीर्वादगळिन्द कार्यसिद्धियागुवन्तॆ हरसिदरु. ॥133 ॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*133. viiryavaan* = the courageous; *haripuN^gavaH* = Hanuma;
*uktvaa* = saying; *iti* = thus; *aalabhya* = touching; *shailam* = the
mountain; *paaNinaa* = with His hand; *aavishhya* = entering sky; *jagaama* =
went; *prahasanniva* = with a smile.

**अनुवादः**

The courageous Hanuma saying thus, touched the mountain with His hand, entered the
sky and flew on with a smile.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Regarded with high esteem (_bahumānād avekṣitaḥ_) by both the mountain and the ocean, and honored, he was hailed with fitting (_upapannābhiḥ_) benedictions (_āśīrbhiḥ_).
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**133–135 (The Embodied Ocean and the Higher Wind-Path)**

Leaving behind both the mountain and the great ocean (हित्वा शैलमहार्णवौ) after receiving their fitting blessings (उपपन्नाभिस्तत्काले युक्ताभिः), Hanūmān climbs to a still higher stratum of the sky (यत्र देशे स्थितस्ततोऽपि बहूर्ध्वम्), entering his father's wind-track (वायुमार्गम्). Govindarāja makes a perceptive observation on हित्वा शैलमहार्णवौ: like Maināka, the Ocean too had assumed an embodied personal form (मूर्तिमन्तौ तौ हित्वेत्यर्थः। अनेन शैलवत्समुद्रोऽपि मूर्तिमत्तया तत्रादृश्यतेति सूचितम्) to witness and bless the hero.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अथोर्ध्वं दूरमागत्य हित्वा शैलमहार्णवौ ।  
पितुः पन्थानमासाद्य जगाम विमलेऽम्बरे ॥ १३४ ॥
</details>

पितुः पन्थानमास्थाय जगाम विमलेऽम्बरे ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

फिर पर्वत और समुद्रको छोड़कर उनसे दूर ऊपर उठकर अपने पिताके मार्गका आश्रय ले हनुमान् जी  निर्मल आकाशमें चलने लगे ॥ १३४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

बळिक मारुतियु पर्वत-समुद्र इब्बरिन्दलू बिळ्कॊण्डु, मेल्मुखवागि बहळ ऎत्तरक्कॆ हारि वायुमार्गवन्नु सेरि आकाशदल्लि प्रयाणवन्नु मुन्दुवरिसिदनु.॥134॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*134. saH* = that; *anilaatmajaH* = Hanuma; *avekshitaH*
= was seen; *bahumaanaat* = with great respect; *parvata samudraabhyaam* = by the
ocean and the mountain; *puujitaH* = and also worshipped; *upapannabhiH aashiirbhiH*
= with suitable blessings.

**अनुवादः**

That Hanuma was thus seen with great respect by the ocean and the mountain, and also
worshipped with suitable blessings.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Then, having risen far upward (_ūrdhvaṃ dūram āgatya_) and left behind the mountain and the great ocean (_śailamahārṇavau_), he gained his father&#x27;s path (_pituḥ panthānam_) and coursed through the spotless sky.
</details>
</details>

वायुसूनुर्निरालम्बो जगाम कपिकुञ्जरः ॥ १३५ ॥

</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

मुन्दॆ-मुन्दॆ इन्नु ऎत्तरक्कॆ हारि मैनाकपर्वतवन्नॆ वीक्षिसुत्ता याव अवलम्बनॆयू इल्लदॆ निर्मलाकाशदल्लि हारिकॊण्डु होगुत्तिद्दनु.॥135॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*135. atha* = after that; *hitvaa* = leaving; *shaila
mahaarNavau* = the mountain and the great ocean; *utplutya* = flying; *uurdhvam*
= up; *duuram* = far; *vimale ambare* = in the clear sky; *jagaama* = went;
*aasthaaya* = following; *panthaanam* = the path; *pituH* = of (His)
father.

**अनुवादः**

After that leaving the mountain and the great ocean, and flying up far into the
clear sky, Hanuma followed the path of his father.

</details>
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्द्वितीयं हनुमतो दृष्ट्वा कर्म सदुष्करम् ।

</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

हनुमान् जी का यह दूसरा अत्यन्त दुष्कर कर्म देखकर सम्पूर्ण देवता, सिद्ध और महर्षिगण उनकी प्रशंसा करने लगे ॥ १३६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

हनुमन्तन अतिदुष्करवाद ऎरडनॆय आ कार्यवन्नु नोडि देवतॆगळू, महर्षिगळू मत्तु सिद्धरू मारुतियन्नु बहळवागि श्लाघिसिदरु. (मॊदलनॆय कर्म पर्वतद तुदियिन्द आगसक्कॆ हारिदुदु. ऎरडनॆयदु मैनाकनन्नु जयिसिदुदु. आगसदल्ले निन्तु मैनाकनन्नु मुट्टि, पर्वतद मेलॆ इळियदॆ पुनः तन्न वेगवन्नु हॆच्चिसिकॊण्डिद्दु. इदु पक्षिगळिगू साध्यवागद कार्यवन्नु पक्षगळिल्लदवनाद हनुमन्तनु माडिदुदु निजवागि महत्कार्यवे आगिदॆ.)॥136॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*136. vaayu suunuH* = the son of Vayu; *praapya* = obtained;
*gatim* = speed; *uurdhvam* = in the upper portion; *bhuuyaH cha* = again;
*avalokayan* = seeing; *tam girim* = that mountain (below); *jagaama* = went;
*vimale ambare* = in the clear sky; *niraalambe* = which had no support.

**अनुवादः**

The son of Vayu obtained the speed to fly still higher into the sky and seeing that
mountain below, went in the clear sky which had no support.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Beholding that second feat (_tad dvitīyaṃ karma_) of Hanūmān, most difficult to accomplish (_suduṣkaram_), all the gods, _siddhas_, and supreme sages praised him (_praśaśaṃsuḥ_).
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**136 (What Was Hanūmān's `Second Feat'?)**

The commentators offer three distinct explanations of why the gods hail this as Hanūmān's `second feat' (तद्द्वितीयं कर्म): Rāma Varmā's _Tilaka_ identifies it as his bounding upward a second time without even resting (अविश्रम्यैव पुनरुत्प्लवनरूपं कर्म); Śivasahāya's _Śiromaṇi_ defines it as his refusal of rest and conquest of the mountain following his initial leap (उत्प्लवनापेक्षया द्वित्वसंख्यापूरकं विश्रान्त्यकरणद्वितीयं तत्पर्वतजयरूपं कर्म); and Govindarāja explains it as second relative to his leaping over the ocean itself (समुद्रप्लवनापेक्षया).
</details>
</details>

देवताश्चाभवन् हृष्टास्तत्रस्थास्तस्य कर्मणा ।  
काञ्चनस्य सुनाभस्य सहस्राक्षश्च वासवः ॥ १३७ ॥
</details>

काञ्चनस्य सुनाभस्य सहस्राक्षश्च वासवः ॥ १३७ ॥

प्रश्शंसुः सुराः सर्वे सिद्धाश्च परमर्षयः ॥ १-५-१३७

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

वहाँ आकाशमें ठहरे हुए देवता तथा सहस्र नेत्रधारी इन्द्र उस सुन्दर मध्य भागवाले सुवर्णमय मैनाक पर्वतके उस कार्यसे बहुत प्रसन्न हुए ॥ १३७ ॥
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

The deities stationed there (_tatrasthāḥ_) were delighted by the action of that golden (_kāñcanasya_), fair-navelled (_sunābhasya_) mountain, as was Thousand-Eyed Vāsava.
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**137–138 (Indra's Reconciliation with Sunābha)**

Witnessing golden Maināka—here called सुनाभः (`having a fair central ridge', शोभनमध्यस्य)—suddenly rise to aid Rāma's envoy, the gods stationed in the sky (तत्रस्था मेनाकस्था गगनस्था वा) and Indra (वासवः) himself rejoice. Overcome with pleasure, Śacī's lord addresses his ancient fugitive in a voice choked with emotion (गद्गदं स्खलद्वाग्विशिष्टं वच उवाच).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

उवाच वचनं धीमान् परितोषात् सगद‍्गदम् ।  
सुनाभं पर्वतश्रेष्ठं स्वयमेव शचीपतिः ॥ १३८ ॥
</details>

सुनाभं पर्वतश्रेष्ठं स्वयमेव शचीपतिः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

उस समय स्वयं बुद्धिमान् शचीपति इन्द्रने अत्यन्त संतुष्ट होकर पर्वतश्रेष्ठ सुनाभ मैनाकसे गद‍्गद वाणीमें कहा— ॥ १३८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

सुवर्णनाभनाद मैनाकनु समुद्रदिन्द मेलॆद्दु हनुमन्तनन्नु सत्करिसलु आह्वानिसिद्दक्कागि आ प्रदेशदल्लिद्द देवतॆगळू हागू साविर कण्णुगळुळ्ळ देवेन्द्रनू परम सन्तोषगॊण्डरु. धीमन्तनाद शचीपतियु अतिसन्तोषदिन्द तानागि गद्गद ध्वनियिन्द काञ्चन शिखरवुळ्ळ मैनाकनिगॆ हीगॆ हेळिदनु ॥137-138॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*137. dR^ishhTvaa* = seeing; *hanumataH* = Hanuma's;
*tat* = that; *sudushhkaram* = very difficult;dvitiiyam = second; *karma* =
task; *sarve* = all; *suraaH* = Devas; *siddhaashcha* = Siddhas and;
*paramarshhayaH* = great sages; *prashashaMsuH* = praised (Him).

**अनुवादः**

Seeing Hanuma perform that very difficult second task (first one being the flight
over ocean), all Devas, Siddhas and great sages praised Him.
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*138. devataashcha* = Devas and; *sahasraakshaH* = the thousand
eyed; *vaasavashcha* = Devendra; *tatrasthaaH* = who were there; *abhavan* =
became; *hR^ishhTaaH* = happy; *karmaNaa* = by the task; *tasya kaaJNchanasya*
= of that golden hued; *sunaabhasya* = Mainaka.

**अनुवादः**

Devas and the thousand eyed Devendra who were there, became happy by the task of
that golden hued Mainaka.
</details>

The wise lord of Śacī (_śacīpatiḥ_) himself, out of deep satisfaction (_paritoṣāt_), spoke words choked with emotion (_sagadgadam_) unto Sunābha, the foremost of mountains:

हिरण्यनाभ शैलेन्द्र परितुष्टोऽस्मि ते भृशम् ।  
अभयं ते प्रयच्छामि तिष्ठ सौम्य यथासुखम् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘सुवर्णमय शैलराज मैनाक! मैं तुमपर बहुत प्रसन्न हूँ । सौम्य! तुम्हें अभय दान देता हूँ । तुम सुखपूर्वक जहाँ चाहो, जाओ ॥ १३९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

‘‘हिरण्यनाभने! शैलश्रेष्ठने! निन्न ई कार्यदिन्द नानु हॆच्चु प्रीतनागिद्देनॆ. निनगॆ अभयवन्नु इत्तिरुवॆनु. नन्निन्द निनगॆ मुन्दॆ याव भयवू इरदु. ई स्थानदल्ले नीनु सुखवागिरु. ॥139॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*139. dhimaan* = the wise; *shachiipatiH* = Devendra;
*svayameva* = himself; *uvaacha* = spoke; *vachanam* = words;
*sagadgadam* = through faltering tones; *paritoshhaat* = due to intense happiness;
*sunaabham* = to Mainaka; *parvata shreshhTham* = the best among mountains.

**अनुवादः**

The wise Devendra himself spoke these words through faltering tones due to extreme
happiness, to Mainaka, the best among mountains.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘O Hiraṇyanābha, lord of mountains, I am exceedingly pleased (_parituṣṭo &#x27;smi_) with you! I grant you immunity from fear (_abhayam_); go, gentle one (_saumya_), at your ease (_yathāsukham_)!’
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**139–141 (Indra's Amnesty and the Paradox of Fearlessness)**

$1e
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

साह्यं कृतं ते सुमहद् विश्रान्तस्य हनूमतः ।  
क्रमतो योजनशतं निर्भयस्य भये सति ॥ १४० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

साह्यं कृतं ते सुमहद् विश्रान्तस्य हनूमतः ।

_________________
साह्यं कृतं त्वया सौम्य विक्रान्तस्य हनूमतः ।  
क्रमतो योजनशतं निर्भयस्य भये सति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘सौ योजन समुद्रको लाँघते समय जिनके मनमें कोई भय नहीं रहा है, फिर भी जिनके लिये हमारे हृदयमें यह भय था कि पता नहीं इनका क्या होगा? उन्हीं हनुमान् जी को विश्रामका अवसर देकर तुमने उनकी बहुत बड़ी सहायता की है ॥ १४० ॥
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘Great assistance (_sāhyaṃ sumahat_) has been rendered by you to the untiring (_viśrāntasya_) Hanūmān as he strides across a hundred _yojanas_, fearless (_nirbhayasya_) even when cause for fear is present (_bhaye sati_).’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

रामस्यैष हितायैव याति दाशरथेः कपिः ।  
सत्क्रियां कुर्वता शक्त्या तोषितोऽस्मि दृढं त्वया ॥ १४१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

रामस्यैष हितायैव याति दाशरथेः कपिः ।  
सत्क्रियां कुर्वता शक्त्या तोषितोऽस्मि दृढं त्वया ॥ १४१ ॥
_________________
रामस्यैष हिदूत्येन याति दाशरथेर्हरिः ।  
सत्क्रियां कुर्वता तस्य तोषितोऽस्मि भृशं त्वया ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

साह्यं ते सुमहद्विक्रान्तस्य हनूमतः ।
क्रमतो योजनशतं निर्भयस्य भये सति ॥ ५-१-१४१
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘ये वानरश्रेष्ठ हनुमान् दशरथनन्दन श्रीरामकी सहायताके लिये ही जा रहे हैं । तुमने यथाशक्ति इनका सत्कार करके मुझे पूर्ण संतोष प्रदान किया है’ ॥ १४१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

भयपडुव सन्दर्भविद्दरू भयविल्लदॆ नूरु योजनगळन्नु हारलु हॊरटिरुव भय रहितनाद, महापराक्रमियाद हनुमन्तनिगॆ नीनु सहायवन्नॆसगलु मुन्दॆ बन्दिरुवॆ. दशरथ नन्दननाद श्रीरामन हितक्कागिये ई मारुतियु लङ्कॆगॆ होगुत्तिद्दानॆ. अन्तहवनन्नु नीनु सत्करिसलु मुन्दॆ बन्दुदक्कागि नानु परम सन्तुष्टनागिरुवॆनु.’’ ॥140-141॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*140. hiraNya naabha* = O Mainaka! shailendra = the king of mountains!
bhR^isham paritushhtaH asmi = I am very happy; *te* = in you; *saumya* = O gentle
one! prayachhaami = I am giving; *abhayam* = protection; *te* = to you; *yathaa
sukham tishhTha* = proceed according to your comfort.

**अनुवादः**

"O Mainaka, the king of mountains! I am very happy with you. O gentle one! I am
giving protection to you. Proceed according to your comfort."
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*141. hanuumataH* = (To) Hanuma; *vikraantasya* = who is
courageous; *kramataH* = crossing; *yojana shatam* = hundred yojanas;
*nirbhayasya* = who is fearless; *bhaye sati* = even when there is reason to be
afraid; *sumahat* = great; *saahyam* = help; *kR^itam* = has been done;
*te* = (by) you.

**अनुवादः**

To the dauntless Hanuma crossing hundred yojanas, who is fearless even when there is
reason to be afraid, a great help has been done by you.
</details>

‘This monkey goes strictly for the welfare (_hitāyaiva_) of Rāma, son of Daśaratha; by offering him hospitality (_satkriyāṃ kurvatā_) to the best of your power (_śaktyā_), you have firmly satisfied me!’
</details>
</details>

ततः प्रहर्षमगमद्विपुलं पर्वतोत्तमः ।

सत्क्रियां कुर्वता तस्य तोषितोऽस्मि दृढं त्वया ॥ ५-१-१४२

देवताओंके स्वामी शतक्रतु इन्द्रको संतुष्ट देखकर पर्वतोंमें श्रेष्ठ मैनाकको बड़ा हर्ष प्राप्त हुआ ॥ १४२ ॥

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

That foremost of mountains experienced boundless joy (_praharṣaṃ vipulam_) upon beholding Śatakratu, the lord of the deities, thoroughly pleased (_parituṣṭam_).
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**142–143 (Maināka's Abiding Stance)**

Rejoicing at Indra's pleasure, Maināka remains अवस्थितः—interpreted by _Tilaka_ as `submerged again beneath the waters' (जलान्तरमग्न एव) and by _Śiromaṇi_ as `standing tall above the sea without fear' (उर्ध्वोदितः). Meanwhile Hanūmān swiftly leaves the Maināka-sector of the ocean behind (मैनाकाधिष्ठितसागरप्रदेशम्).
</details>
</details>

हनूमांश्च मुहूर्तेन व्यतिचक्राम सागरम् ॥ १४३ ॥
</details>

</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

इस प्रकार इन्द्रका दिया हुआ वर पाकर मैनाक उस समय जलमें स्थित हो गया और हनुमान् जी समुद्रके उस प्रदेशको उसी मुहूर्तमें लाँघ गये ॥ १४३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

समुद्रवासक्कॆ कारणनाद देवतॆगळ ऒडॆयनाद देवेन्द्रनु तन्न विषयदल्लि परितुष्टनादनॆन्दु तिळिदु मैनाकनु परमहृष्टनादनु. देवेन्द्रनिन्द अभयवन्नु पडॆदुकॊण्डु मैनाकनु यावुदे भयविल्लदॆ तानु हिन्दिद्द स्थळदल्लिये सुस्थिरवागि निन्तनु. इत्त हनुमन्तनु आ सागर प्रदेशवन्नु ऒन्दु क्षण कालदल्लि अतिक्रमिसि मुन्दॆ सागिदनु.॥142-143॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*142. eshhaH hariH* = this Vanara; *yaati* = is going;
*hitaayaiva* = for the benefit; *raamasya* = of Sri Rama; *daasaratheH* = the
son of Dasaratha; *dR^idham toshhitaH asmi* = I am very happy; *tvayaa* = with you;
*kurvataa* = (who is) doing; *satkriyaam* = respect; *tasya* = to Him.

**अनुवादः**

This Vanara Hanuma is going for the benefit of Sri Rama, the son of Dasaratha. I am
very happy with you who is doing respect to Him.
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*143. tatH* = thereafter; *parvatottamaH* = the best among
mountains; *agamat* = obtained; *vipulam* = great; *praharshham* = happiness;
*dR^ishhTvaa* = seeing; *shatakratum* = Devendra; *patim* = lord;
*devataanaam* = of Devas; *paritushhTam* = being happy.

**अनुवादः**

Thereafter, the best among mountains obtained great happiness on seeing Devendra,
the lord of Devas being happy.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Having been granted that boon (_dattavaraḥ_), that mountain then remained securely in place (_avasthitaḥ_), and within an instant (_muhūrtena_) Hanūmān strode beyond (_vyaticakrāma_) that stretch of the ocean.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ततो देवाः सगन्धर्वाः सिद्धाश्च परमर्षयः ।  
अब्रुवन् सूर्यसङ्काशां सुरसां नागमातरम् ॥ १४४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ततो देवाः सगन्धर्वाः सिद्धाश्च परमर्षयः ।  
अब्रुवन् सूर्यसङ्काशां सुरसां नागमातरम् ॥ १४४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

स वै दत्तवरः शैलो बभूवावस्थितस्तदा ।
हनुमांश्च मुहूर्तेन व्यतिचक्राम सागरम् ॥ ५-१-१४४
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

तब देवता, गन्धर्व, सिद्ध और महर्षियोंने सूर्यतुल्य तेजस्विनी नागमाता सुरसासे कहा— ॥ १४४ ॥
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Then the gods together with the _gandharvas_, _siddhas_, and supreme sages spoke to Surasā, the mother of the _nāgas_ (_nāgamātaram_), who shone like the sun (_sūryasaṃkāśām_):
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**144 (The Commissioning of Surasā)**

As _Tilaka_ explains, because Hanūmān is about to enter Rāvaṇa's demon-infested citadel, the celestials now dispatch the serpent-mother Surasā to test his resourcefulness and fitness for the mission (अथ देवैर्लङ्कां प्रविश्य हनुमतः कार्यक्षमत्वपरीक्षणार्थं सुरसाप्रवर्तनम्).
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Prompted by the gods to test whether Hanūmān will prevail by stratagem or despair, Surasā rises from mid-ocean in a monstrous rākṣasa form and commands him to enter her jaws.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अयं वातात्मजः श्रीमान् प्लवते सागरोपरि ।  
हनूमान् नाम तस्य त्वं मुहूर्तं विघ्नमाचर ॥ १४५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अयं वातात्मजः श्रीमान् प्लवते सागरोपरि ।  
हनूमान् नाम तस्य त्वं मुहूर्तं विघ्नमाचर ॥ १४५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

ततो देवाः सगन्धर्वाः सिद्धाश्च परमर्षयः ।
अब्रूवन् सूर्यसंकाशां सुरसां नागमातरम् ॥ ५-१-१४५
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘ये पवननन्दन श्रीमान् हनुमान् जी  समुद्रके ऊपर होकर जा रहे हैं । तुम दो घड़ीके लिये इनके मार्गमें विघ्न डाल दो ॥ १४५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*145. tataH* = after that; *devaaH* = Devas;
*sagandharvaaH* = together with Gandharvas; *siddhaashcha* = Siddhas;
*paramarshhayaH* = and great sages; *abruvan* = spoke (thus); *surasaam* = to
Surasa; *naagamaataram* = mother of the Nagas; *suuryasaMkaasham* = equal to the Sun
(in brilliance).

**अनुवादः**

Thereafter, Devas together with Gandharvas, Siddhas and great sages spoke thus to
Surasa, the mother of Nagas, equal to the Sun in brilliance.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘This glorious son of the Wind (_vātātmajaḥ_), named Hanūmān, is leaping across the ocean; create an obstacle (_vighnam ācara_) for him for a brief moment (_muhūrtam_)!’
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**145–147 (Testing Both Might and Wit)**

The gods ask Surasā to obstruct Hanūmān momentarily (मुहूर्तं विघ्नमाचर) by assuming a mountain-sized demoness form with a sky-scraping maw—where Rāma Varmā's _Tilaka_ notes the epic neuter shortening in नभःस्पृशमिति ह्रस्वमार्षम्. Commenting on 5.1.147ab, _Tilaka_ explains that बलम् denotes both physical might and tactical ingenuity (काकबलमुपायबलं च), while Śivasahāya's _Śiromaṇi_ distinguishes बलम् (`strength of vital breath and senses', प्राणेन्द्रिययोः सामर्थ्यम्) from पराक्रमम् (`enemy-crushing valor', शत्रुविघातकं शौर्यम्). Will he overcome her by a clever stratagem (उपायेन) or succumb to despondency (विषादम्)?
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

राक्षसं रूपमास्थाय सुघोरं पर्वतोपमम् ।  
दंष्ट्राकरालं पिङ्गाक्षं वक्त्रं कृत्वा नभःस्पृशम् ॥ १४६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

राक्षसं रूपमास्थाय सुघोरं पर्वतोपमम् ।  
दंष्ट्राकरालं पिङ्गाक्षं वक्त्रं कृत्वा नभःस्पृशम् ॥ १४६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘तुम पर्वतके समान अत्यन्त भयंकर राक्षसीका रूप धारण करो । उसमें विकराल दाढ़ें, पीले नेत्र और आकाशको स्पर्श करनेवाला विकट मुँह बनाओ ॥ १४६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

बळिक गन्धर्वरू, देवतॆगळू, सिद्धरू, महर्षिगळू, सूर्यसदृशळाद नागमातॆयाद सुरसॆयन्नु कण्डु हेळिदरु ‘‘वायुनन्दननू तेजस्वियू आद कपिश्रेष्ठ हनुमन्तनु समुद्रद मेलॆ हारिहोगुत्तिद्दानॆ. अवनिगॆ नीनु क्षणकाल विघ्नवन्नुण्टुमाडु. पर्वतोपमवाद घोरवाद राक्षसिय रूपवन्नु धरिसि, कॊरॆदाडॆगळिन्द भयङ्करवागि काणुव, कन्दु-हळदि बण्णद कण्णुगळिन्द कूडिरुव, आकाशवन्नु मुट्टुव मुखदिन्द कूडि अवन गमनक्कॆ विघ्नवन्नुण्टु माडु.॥144-146॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*144. tadaa* = then; *saH* = that; *shailaH* = mountain;
*datta varaH* = with a given boon; *babhuuva* = became; *avasthitaH* =
situated there; *hanumaamshcha* = Hanuma also; *vyatichakraama* = crossed;
*saagaram* = (over that portion of) the ocean; *muhuurtena* = in an instant.

**अनुवादः**

Then that mountain with a given boon from Devendra stood still there. Hanuma also
leaped over that part of the ocean in an instant.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘Assuming a most dreadful _rākṣasa_ form (_rākṣasaṃ rūpam_), huge as a mountain, and making your face terrible with fangs (_daṃṣṭrākarālam_), tawny-eyed (_piṅgākṣam_), and sky-touching (_nabhaḥspṛśam_)—’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

बलमिच्छामहे ज्ञातुं भूयश्चास्य पराक्रमम् ।  
त्वां विजेष्यत्युपायेन विषादं वा गमिष्यति ॥ १४७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

बलमिच्छामहे ज्ञातुं भूयश्चास्य पराक्रमम् ।  
त्वां विजेष्यत्युपायेन विषादं वा गमिष्यति ॥ १४७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘हमलोग पुनः हनुमान् जी के बल और पराक्रमकी परीक्षा लेना चाहते हैं । या तो किसी उपायसे ये तुम्हें जीत लेंगे अथवा विषादमें पड़ जायँगे (इससे इनके बलाबलका ज्ञान हो जायगा)’ ॥ १४७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ई कपीश्वरन बलवन्नु पराक्रमवन्नु तिळियबयसिद्देवॆ. इवनु उपायान्तरगळिन्द निन्नन्नु जयिसुत्तानो अथवा विषादपडुत्तानो नोडोण.’’॥147॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

146; *147. ayam* = this; *shriimaan* = glorious one;
*vaataatmajaH* = son of Vayu; *hanumaannaama* = with the name of Hanuma;
*plavate* = is flying; *saagaropari* = on the ocean; *tvam* = you;
*aasthaaya* = obtain; *sughoram* = a horrible; *parvatopamam* = mountain like;
*raakshasam* = demonic; *ruupam* = appearance; *kR^itvaa* = (and) make;
*vaktram* = a face; *daMshhTraakaraaLam* = (with) fearful tusks;
*piN^gaaksham* = red-brown eyes; *nabham samam* = and (vast) like the sky;
*aachara* = (and) create; *vighnam* = an obstacle; *tasya* = to Him;
*muhuurtam* = for an instant.

**अनुवादः**

"This glorious son of Vayu, going by the name of Hanuma is flying over the ocean.
You obtain a horrible mountain like demonic appearance and make a face with fearful tusks,
red-brown eyes and as vast as the sky, and create an obstacle to Him for an instant."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘We wish to ascertain (_jñātum_) his strength and further his valor (_parākramam_): whether he will conquer you by a stratagem (_upāyena_) or sink into despair (_viṣādaṃ vā gamiṣyati_)!’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एवमुक्ता तु सा देवी दैवतैरभिसत्कृता ।  
समुद्रमध्ये सुरसा बिभ्रती राक्षसं वपुः ॥ १४८ ॥  
विकृतं च विरूपं च सर्वस्य च भयावहम् ।  
प्लवमानं हनूमन्तमावृत्येदमुवाच ह ॥ १४९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एवमुक्ता तु सा देवी दैवतैरभिसत्कृता ।  
समुद्रमध्ये सुरसा बिभ्रती राक्षसं वपुः ॥ १४८ ॥  
विकृतं च विरूपं च सर्वस्य च भयावहम् ।  
प्लवमानं हनूमन्तमावृत्येदमुवाच ह ॥ १४९ ॥
_________________
एवमुक्ता तु सा देवि दैवतैरभिसत्कृता ।  
समुद्रमध्ये सुरसा बिभ्रती राक्षसं वपुः ॥
विकृतं च विरूपं च सर्वस्य च भयावहम् ।  
प्लवमानं हनूमन्तमावृत्येदमुवाच ह ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

देवताओंके सत्कारपूर्वक इस प्रकार कहनेपर देवी सुरसाने समुद्रके बीचमें राक्षसीका रूप धारण किया । उसका वह रूप बड़ा ही विकट, बेडौल और सबके लिये भयावना था । वह समुद्रके पार जाते हुए हनुमान् जी को घेरकर उनसे इस प्रकार बोली— ॥ १४८-१४९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ई विधवागि देवतॆगळिन्द सत्कृतळाद सुरसॆयु अवरु हेळिदन्तॆ, समुद्र मध्यदल्लि विकृतवाद राक्षसिय रूपवन्नान्तु निन्तुकॊण्डळु.॥148॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*148. icchaamahe* = (We are) desiring; *jJNaatum* = to find out;
*asya* = (if) by His; *balam* = strength; *bhuuyaH* = and also;
*paraakramam cha* = and courage; *upaayena* = and intelligence; *vijeshhyati*
= (can He) win; *tvaam* = you; *vaa* = or; *gamishhyati* = get;
*vishhadam* = sorrow.

**अनुवादः**

"We are desiring to find out if by His strength and also courage and intelligence He
can win over you or obtain sorrow."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**148:** Thus addressed and honored (_abhisatkṛtā_) by the deities, that goddess Surasā, wearing a _rākṣasa_ body (_bibhratī rākṣasaṃ vapuḥ_) in the midst of the ocean—

**149:** Deformed (_vikṛtam_), hideous (_virūpam_), and terrifying to all (_sarvasya ca bhayāvaham_)—blocked (_āvṛtya_) the leaping Hanūmān and spoke this word:
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**148–149 (The Monstrous Apparition)**

Honored by the gods, Surasā rises from mid-ocean wearing a hideous _rākṣasa_ form—glossed in _Śiromaṇi_ as deformed with mutilated nose and ears (छिन्ननासिकाकर्णाद्यनेकविकारविशिष्टम्) and contrary to auspicious beauty (शुभरूपाद्विरुद्धम्). _Tilaka_ records the striking variant भयस्य च भयावहम् (`terrifying even to the fear of all worlds').
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

मम भक्ष्यः प्रदिष्टस्त्वमीश्वरैर्वानरर्षभ ।  
अहं त्वां भक्षयिष्यामि प्रविशेदं ममाननम् ॥ १५० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

मम भक्ष्यः प्रदिष्टस्त्वमीश्वरैर्वानरर्षभ ।  
अहं त्वां भक्षयिष्यामि प्रविशेदं ममाननम् ॥ १५० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘कपिश्रेष्ठ! देवेश्वरोंने तुम्हें मेरा भक्ष्य बताकर मुझे अर्पित कर दिया है, अतः मैं तुम्हें खाऊँगी । तुम मेरे इस मुँहमें चले आओ ॥ १५० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ऎल्लरिगू भयवन्नुण्टुमाडुव विकारवाद, घोर राक्षसियागि सुरसादेवियु आकाशमार्गवागि हारिहोगुत्तिद्द हनुमन्तन मार्गवन्नु अड्डगट्टि इन्तॆन्दळु ‘‘वानर श्रेष्ठने! देवतॆगळु निन्नन्नु ननगॆ आहारवन्नागि कळिसिकॊट्टिरुत्तारॆ. नानु निन्नन्नु भक्षिसुवॆनु. ई नन्न मुखदल्लि ईगले प्रवेशिसु. ब्रह्मने हिन्दॆ ननगॆ ई वरवन्नु दयपालिसिरुवनु.’’ ॥149-150॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*149;150. saa devii* = that Goddess; *surasaa* = Surasa;
*evam* = thus; *uktaa* = spoken to; *daivataiH* = by Devas;
*abhisatkR^itaa* = and also respected (by them); *bibhratii* = wearing;
*vikR^itam* = a horrible; *viruupam cha* = disfigured; *raakshasam vapuH* =
appearance of a demon; *samudra madhye* = in the middle of the ocean; *bhayaavaham* =
causing fear; *sarvasya* = to all; *aavR^itya* = and stopping; *hanuumantam* =
Hanuma; *plavantam* = who was flying; *uvaacha ha* = (and) spoke; *idam* =
these words.

**अनुवादः**

That Goddess Surasa, thus spoken to by Devas and also being respected by them,
wearing a horrible disfigured appearance of a demon in the middle of the ocean which caused fear to
all, and stopping Hanuma who was flying, spoke these words.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘You have been assigned (_pradiṣṭaḥ_) by the lords (_īśvaraiḥ_) as my food (_mama bhakṣyaḥ_), O bull among monkeys! I shall devour you (_tvāṃ bhakṣayiṣyāmi_)—enter this mouth of mine!’
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**150 (The Claim of Divine Allotment)**

Blocking Hanūmān's path (आवृत्य रुद्ध्वा), Surasā announces that the gods (ईश्वरैर्देवैः) have allotted him as her lawful prey (मम भक्ष्यः प्रदिष्टः) and commands him to walk straight into her jaws.
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

When Surasā gapes her jaws citing Brahmā's ancient boon, Hanūmān cheerfully recounts Rāma's mission and promises to return to her mouth after beholding Sītā, yet she refuses passage.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

वर एष पुरा दत्तो मम धात्रेति सत्वरा ।  
व्यादाय वक्त्रं विपुलं स्थिता सा मारुतेः पुरः ॥ १५१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

वर एष पुरा दत्तो मम धात्रेति सत्वरा ।  
व्यादाय वक्त्रं विपुलं स्थिता सा मारुतेः पुरः ॥ १५१ ॥
_________________
एवमुक्तः सुरसया प्राञ्जलिर्वानरर्षभः ।  
प्रहृष्टवदनः श्रीमान् इदं वचनमब्रवीत् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

मम भक्षः प्रदिष्टस्त्वमीश्वरैर्वानरर्षभ ।
अहं त्वा भक्षयिष्यामि प्रविशेदं ममाननम् ॥ ५-१-१५१
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘पूर्वकालमें ब्रह्माजीने मुझे यह वर दिया था ।’ ऐसा कहकर वह तुरंत ही अपना विशाल मुँह फैलाकर हनुमान् जी के सामने खड़ी हो गयी ॥ १५१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

हीगॆ हेळि आहारक्कागि अवसरिसुत्तिद्द सुरसॆयु बायन्नु अगलवागि तॆरॆदुकॊण्डु हनुमन्तन मुम्भागदल्लि निन्तळु. आग दिव्य शोभॆयिन्द कङ्गॊळिसुव कपिश्रेष्ठ हनुमन्तनु प्रसन्न मुखभावदिन्द कैजोडिसिकॊण्डु आकॆगॆ हेळिदनु ॥151॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*151. vaanararshhabha* = O best among Vanaras! tvam = you;
*pradishhTaH* = have been given; *eesvaraiH* = by Devas; *mama* = (as) my;
*bhakshaH* = food; *aham* = I; *bhakshayishhyaami* = will eat; *tvaam*
= you; *pravisha* = enter; *idam* = (in) this; *mama* = my; *aananam* =
face.

**अनुवादः**

"O best among Vanaras! You have been given by Devas as my food. I will eat you.
Enter inside my mouth."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘This boon (_vara eṣaḥ_) was granted to me of old by the Creator (_dhātrā_)!’—so saying, in haste (_satvarā_), she opened wide her capacious mouth (_vaktraṃ vipulam_) and stood before Māruti.
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**151–153 (Hanūmān's Cheerful Poise and Summary of the Epic)**

$21
</details>
</details>

रामो दाशरथिर्नाम प्रविष्टो दण्डकावनम् ।  
लक्ष्मणेन सह भ्रात्रा वैदेह्या चापि भार्यया ॥ १५२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एवमुक्तः सुरसया प्रहृष्टवदनोऽब्रवीत् ।  
रामो दाशरथिर्नाम प्रविष्टो दण्डकावनम् ।  
लक्ष्मणेन सह भ्रात्रा वैदेह्या चापि भार्यया ॥ १५२ ॥
_________________
रामो दाशरथिः श्रीमान् प्रविष्टो दण्डकावनम् ।  
लक्ष्मणेन सह भ्रात्रा वैदेह्या चापि भार्यया ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

एवमुक्तः सुरसया प्राञ्जलिर्वानरर्षभः ।
प्रहृष्टवदनः श्रीमानिदं वचनमब्रवीत् ॥ १-५-१५२
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

सुरसाके ऐसा कहनेपर हनुमान् जी ने प्रसन्नमुख होकर कहा—‘देवि! दशरथनन्दन श्रीरामचन्द्रजी अपने भाई लक्ष्मण और धर्मपत्नी सीताजीके साथ दण्डकारण्यमें आये थे ॥ १५२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

‘ताये! दशरथमहाराजन कुमारनाद श्रीरामनु तम्मनाद लक्ष्मण हागू भार्यॆयाद सीतादेवियॊन्दिगॆ दण्डकारण्यक्कॆ बन्दनु. ॥152॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*152. evam* = thus; *uktaH* = spoken to; *surasayaa* = by
Surasa; *shriimaan* = the glorious; *vaanararshhabhaH* = best among Vanaras;
*prahR^ishhTa vadanaH* = with a happy face; *praaJNaliH* = (and) with folded hands;
*abraviit* = spoke; *idam vachanam* = this sentence.

**अनुवादः**

Thus spoken to by Surasa, the glorious Hanuma, with a happy face and with folded
hands, spoke these words to Surasa.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Thus addressed by Surasā, he of joyful countenance (_prahṛṣṭavadanaḥ_) spoke: ‘Rāma, the son of Daśaratha by name, entered the Daṇḍaka forest (_daṇḍakāvanam_) together with his brother Lakṣmaṇa and his wife Vaidehī.’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अन्यकार्यविषक्तस्य बद्धवैरस्य राक्षसैः ।  
तस्य सीता हृता भार्या रावणेन यशस्विनी ॥ १५३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अन्यकार्यविषक्तस्य बद्धवैरस्य राक्षसैः ।  
तस्य सीता हृता भार्या रावणेन यशस्विनी ॥ १५३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘वहाँ परहित-साधनमें लगे हुए श्रीरामका राक्षसोंके साथ वैर बँध गया । अतः रावणने उनकी यशस्विनी भार्या सीताको हर लिया ॥ १५३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

श्रीरामन भार्यॆयाद, यशस्विनियाद सीतादेवियन्नु रावणनॆम्ब राक्षसनु अपहरिसिकॊण्डु होगिद्दानॆ.॥153॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*153. daasharathiH* = the son of Dasaratha; *raamo naama* = with
the name of Rama; *pravishhTaH* = entered; *daNDakaavanam* = the forest of Dandaka;
*saha* = together with; *bhraatraa* = His brother; *lakshmaNena* = Lakshmana;
*bharyayaa* = (and with His) wife; *vaidehyaa* = Seetha.

**अनुवादः**

"The son of Dasaratha, with the name of Rama entered the forest of Dandaka together
with His brother Lakshmana and wife Seetha."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘While he was absorbed in another task (_anyakāryaviṣaktasya_) and locked in enmity (_baddhavairasya_) with the _rākṣasas_, his illustrious wife Sītā was abducted (_hṛtā_) by Rāvaṇa.’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तस्याः सकाशं दूतोऽहं गमिष्ये रामशासनात् ।  
कर्तुमर्हसि रामस्य साह्यं विषयवासिनि ॥ १५४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तस्याः सकाशं दूतोऽहं गमिष्ये रामशासनात् ।  
कर्तुमर्हसि रामस्य साह्यं विषयवासिनि ॥ १५४ ॥
_________________
तस्याः सकाशं दूतोऽहं गमिष्ये रामशासनात् ।  
कर्तुमर्हसि रामस्य साह्यं विषयवासिनी ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

अन्यकार्यविषक्तस्य बद्धवैरस्य राक्षसैः ।
तस्य सीत हृता भार्या रावणेन यशस्विनी ॥ ५-१-१५४
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘मैं श्रीरामकी आज्ञासे उनका दूत बनकर सीताजीके पास जा रहा हूँ । तुम भी श्रीरामके राज्यमें निवास करती हो । अतः तुम्हें उनकी सहायता करनी चाहिये ॥ १५४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

नानु श्रीरामन दूतनु. नन्न स्वामियाद श्रीरामन आज्ञानुसारवागि सीतादेविय बळिगॆ होगुत्तिद्देनॆ. धर्मात्मनाद श्रीरामन आळ्विकॆयल्लि वासिसुत्तिरुव नीनु ई समयदल्लि अवन कार्यक्कॆ सहकारवन्नु नीडबेकु.॥154॥
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘At Rāma&#x27;s command (_rāmaśāsanāt_) I am going as an envoy (_dūtaḥ_) to her presence; you ought to render assistance (_sāhyam_) to Rāma, O dweller in his realm (_viṣayavāsini_)!’
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**154–155 (The Appeal to Subjecthood and the Vow of Return)**

Hanūmān first appeals to Surasā as a subject residing within Rāma's imperial domain (रामस्य विषयवासिनि), since Rāma is the sovereign lord of the entire earth (तस्य सर्वाधिपतित्वात्, _Tilaka_; रामराज्यवासिनी, Govindarāja). Failing that, like a true satyavādin, he pledges (सत्यं प्रतिशृणोमि ते) to behold Sītā, report the news back to Rāma (रामं च दृष्ट्वा वृत्तान्तं च निवेद्येति शेषः), and then return to enter her jaws.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अथवा मैथिलीं दृष्ट्वा रामं चाक्लिष्टकारिणम् ।  
आगमिष्यामि ते वक्त्रं सत्यं प्रतिशृणोमि ते ॥ १५५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अथवा मैथिलीं दृष्ट्वा रामं चाक्लिष्टकारिणम् ।  
आगमिष्यामि ते वक्त्रं सत्यं प्रतिशृणोमि ते ॥ १५५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तस्याः सकाशं दूतोऽहं गमिष्ये रामकारणात् ।
कर्तुमर्हसि रामस्य साह्यं विषयवासिनि ॥ ५-१-१५५
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘अथवा (यदि तुम मुझे खाना ही चाहती हो तो) मैं सीताजीका दर्शन करके अनायास ही महान् कर्म करनेवाले श्रीरामचन्द्रजीसे जब मिल लूँगा, तब तुम्हारे मुखमें आ जाऊँगा—यह तुमसे सच्ची प्रतिज्ञा करके कहता हूँ’ ॥ १५५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ब्रह्मनित्तिरुव वरदन्तॆ नानु निनगॆ आहारवाग बेकन्नुवुदादरॆ, सीतादेवियन्नु नोडि, आकॆय कुशल वार्तॆयन्नु कार्यनिर्वहणादक्षनाद श्रीरामनिगॆ तिळिसि, बळिक नानु निन्न बायिगॆ बीळुत्तेनॆ. ई मातन्नु नानु सत्यद मेलॆ आणॆयिट्टु हेळुत्तेनॆ.’’॥154-155॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*154. tasya* = that Sri Rama; *baddha vairasya* = with a bitter
enmity; *raakshasaiH* = with Rakshasas. anya kaarya vishhaktasya = being diverted in another
task; *bharyaa* = His wife; *siitaa* = Seetha; *yashasvinii* = the famous one;
*hR^itaa* = had been stolen; *raavaNena* = by Ravana.

**अनुवादः**

"When that Sri Rama, with a bitter enmity towards Rakshasas, was diverted in another
task, His famed wife Seetha had been taken away by Ravana."
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*155. aham* = I; *gamishhye* = can go; *raama kaaraNaat*
= for Rama's sake; *duutaH* = as a messenger; *tasyaaH* = (to find) Her;
*sakaasham* = presence; *vishhaya vaasinii* = O subject (of Sri Rama)! arhasi = (you
are) suited; *kartum* = to do; *saahyam* = help; *raamasya* = of Rama.

**अनुवादः**

"I am going for Rama's sake as a messenger to find Her presence. O subject of Sri
Rama! You are suited to help Rama."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘Or else (_athavā_), having seen Maithilī and Rāma of unwearying deeds (_akliṣṭakāriṇam_), I shall return to your mouth—I solemnly promise you (_satyaṃ pratiśṛṇomi te_)!’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एवमुक्ता हनुमता सुरसा कामरूपिणी ।  
अब्रवीन्नातिवर्तेन्मां कश्चिदेष वरो मम ॥ १५६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एवमुक्ता हनुमता सुरसा कामरूपिणी ।  
अब्रवीन्नातिवर्तेन्मां कश्चिदेष वरो मम ॥ १५६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

हनुमान् जी के ऐसा कहनेपर इच्छानुसार रूप धारण करनेवाली सुरसा बोली—‘मुझे यह वर मिला है कि कोई भी मुझे लाँघकर आगे नहीं जा सकता’ ॥ १५६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

हनुमन्तनु हीगॆ हेळलु कामरूपिणियाद सुरसॆयु पुनः इन्तॆन्दळु ‘‘वानरेश्वरने! नन्नन्नु अतिक्रमिसि यारू होगलाररु. इन्तह अमोघवाद वरवन्नु नानु पडॆदिरुवॆनु.’’ ॥156॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*156. athavaa* = otherwise; *dR^ishhTvaa* = (after seeing);
*maithiliim* = Seetha; *raamam cha* = and Rama; *aklishhTa kaariNam* = who
makes (Seetha) without troubles; *aagamishhyaami* = (I will) obtain; *te* = your;
*vaktram* = face; *pratishR^iNomi* = I am promising; *satyam* = truthfully;
*te* = to you.

**अनुवादः**

"Otherwise after seeing Seetha and informing that to Sri Rama who makes Seetha
without troubles, I will obtain your mouth. I am promising truthfully to you."
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Thus addressed by Hanūmān, Surasā, able to assume any form at will (_kāmarūpiṇī_), said: ‘None may pass me by (_nātivarten mām_)—this is my boon (_varo mama_)!’
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**156 (The Inexorable Boon)**

Unmoved, the shape-shifting Surasā insists that no one may pass without entering her mouth (नातिवर्तेन्मां ममाननमप्रविश्य न गच्छेत्), where both _Tilaka_ and Govindarāja note the _ārṣa_ active optative अतिवर्तेत् (परस्मैपदमार्षम्).
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Insisting that Hanūmān must enter her mouth today, Surasā matches his expanding bulk as they swell from ten and twenty yojanas to thirty and forty across the sky.
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*157. evam* = thus; *uktaa* = spoken to; *hanumataa* = by
Hanuma; *surasaa* = Surasa; *kaamaruupiNii* = with power to take desired form;
*abraviit* = spoke (thus); *kashchit* = No one; *naativarteta* = can overcome
(me); *eshhaH* = this; *mama* = (is) my; *varaH* = boon.

**अनुवादः**

Thus being spoken to by Hanuma, Surasa with power to take desired form, spoke thus:
"No one can overcome me. This is my boon."

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Beholding him setting forth (_taṃ prayāntam_), Surasā, the mother of the _nāgas_, desiring to test (_jijñāsamānā_) Hanūmān&#x27;s strength, spoke these words:
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**157–159 (The Crucial Literal Clue in Surasā's Demand)**

Even as Hanūmān turns to proceed after offering his promise of return (वरदानं कृत्वाऽग्रे प्रयाणोन्मुखम्), Surasā blocks him again with the exact wording of her boon: `Only after entering my mouth today may you go (निविश्य वदनं मेऽद्य गन्तव्यम्)!' Rāma Varmā's _Tilaka_ highlights the decisive implication of her words: she stipulates only that he must enter her mouth before departing—not that he must remain inside or be chewed (प्रविश्यैव गन्तव्यं यदि शक्तिरस्तीत्यर्थः)! That verbal loophole gives Hanūmān his winning strategy.
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

सुरसाके ऐसा कहनेपर वानरशिरोमणि हनुमान् जी  कुपित हो उठे और बोले—‘तुम अपना मुँह इतना बड़ा बना लो जिससे उसमें मेरा भार सह सको’ यों कहकर जब वे मौन हुए, तब सुरसाने अपना मुख दस योजन विस्तृत बना लिया । यह देखकर कुपित हुए हनुमान् जी भी तत्काल दस योजन बड़े हो गये । उन्हें मेघके समान दस योजन विस्तृत शरीरसे युक्त हुआ देख सुरसाने भी अपने मुखको बीस योजन बड़ा बना लिया ॥ १५९—१६१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ओ वानरोत्तमा! नन्न मुखवन्नु प्रवेशिसिये नीनु मुन्दॆ होगबेकु. हिन्दॆ ब्रह्मदेवरु ननगॆ वर कॊट्टिरुवनु. हीगॆ हेळि सुरसॆयु अवसरदिन्द तन्न बायन्नु अगलवागि तॆरॆदुकॊण्डु मारुतिय सम्मुखदल्लि निन्तुकॊण्डळु. अदरिन्द क्रुद्धनाद हनुमन्तनु इन्तॆन्दनु.॥158-159॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*159;160. vaanarottama* = O best among Vanaras! adya = now;
*gantavyam* = (you) have to go; *pravishya* = after entering; *me* = my;
*vadanam* = face; *eshhaH varaH* = this boon; *dattaH* = had been given;
*mama* = to me; *puraa* = long back; *dhaatraa* = by Brahma; *iti* =
(speaking) thus; *satvaraa* = with quickness; *saa* = she; *vyaadaaya* =
opened; *vipulam vaktram* = wide mouth; *sthitaa* = (and) stood; *puraH* =
before; *maaruteH* = Hanuma.

**अनुवादः**

"O best among Vanaras! You are bound to go only after entering my mouth. This boon
had been given to me long ago by Brahma." - speaking thus, with quickness she opened her wide mouth
and stood before Hanuma.
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*161. dR^ishhTvaa* = seeing; *tam* = Him; *megha
saMkaasham* = equal to a cloud; *dasha yojanam aayatam* = ten yojanas long; *surasaa
cha* = Surasa also; *chakaara* = made; *aasyam* = (Her) mouth; *vimshadyojanam
aayatam* = twenty yojanas long.

**अनुवादः**

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**159:** Opening wide her capacious mouth, she stood before Māruti. Thus addressed by Surasā, the bull among monkeys, waxing wroth (_kruddhaḥ_),

**160:** Spoke: ‘Make a mouth wide enough whereby you can contain me (_yena māṃ viṣahiṣyasi_)!’ Having spoken thus to Surasā, whose mouth gaped ten _yojanas_ wide (_daśayojanam āyatām_), in wrath—

**161:** Hanūmān then expanded to a breadth of ten _yojanas_ (_daśayojanavistāraḥ_). Seeing him cloud-like and ten _yojanas_ wide, Surasā too made her maw twenty _yojanas_ wide (_viṃśadyojanam āyatam_).
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**160–161 (The Contest of Expansion Begins)**

Feigning anger (क्रुद्धः), Hanūmān challenges her to open a mouth large enough to hold him (येन यादृशेन मां विषहिष्यसि ग्रहीष्यसि तादृशं वक्त्रं कुरु). Both _Tilaka_ and Śivasahāya's _Śiromaṇi_ construe the feminine accusative दशयोजनमायताम् as qualifying Surasā, whose jaws already gaped ten _yojanas_ wide (दशयोजनविस्तृतमुखां सुरसाम्). When Hanūmān matches her ten-_yojana_ span, she doubles her maw to twenty _yojanas_.
</details>
</details>

तब हनुमान् जी ने क्रुद्ध होकर अपने शरीरको तीस योजन अधिक बढ़ा दिया । फिर तो सुरसाने भी अपने मुँहको चालीस योजन ऊँचा कर लिया ॥ १६२ ॥

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

‘‘ऎलै सुरसा! हागादरॆ नन्न भारवन्नु सहिसलु शक्यवागुवन्तॆ हागू नन्नन्नु नुङ्गलु साध्यवागुवन्तॆ निन्न बायन्नु अगलवागि तॆरॆदुको.’’ ऒडनॆये सुरसॆयु तन्न बायन्नु अगलवागि तॆरॆदुकॊण्डळु. कपिश्रेष्ठनाद हनुमन्तनु कुपितनागि हत्तु योजनगळष्टु विस्तारवागि बॆळॆदनु. मेघसदृशनाद हनुमन्तनु हत्तु योजनगळष्टु विस्तारवागि बॆळॆदिरुवुदन्नु कण्डु सुरसॆयु इप्पत्तु योजनगळष्टु अगलवागि बायितॆरॆदळु. ऒडनॆये हनुमन्तनु मूवत्तु योजनगळष्टु बॆळॆदनु. अदन्नु नोडि सुरसॆयु तन्न मुखवन्नु नलवत्तु योजन विस्तारवागि तॆरॆदुकॊण्डळु.॥160-162॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*160. tadaa* = then; *hanumaan* = Hanuma; *iti uktvaa* =
speaking thus; *surasaam* = to Surasa; *kruddhaH* = became angry; *babhuuva* =
and became; *dasha yojanam* = ten yojanas; *aayataH* = long; *dasha yojana
vistaaraH* = (and) ten yojanas wide.

**अनुवादः**

Then Hanuma speaking thus to Surasa, became angry and became ten yojanas long and
ten yojanas wide.
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*162. tataH* = thereafter; *hanumaaMstu* = Hanuma;
*kruddhaH* = became angry; *triMshadyojanam aayataH* = became thirty yojanas long;
*surasaa* = Surasa; *chakaara* = made; *vaktram* = mouth;
*chatvaariMshat* = forty yojanas; *ucchritam* = high; *viiraH* = the
courageous; *hanumaan* = Hanuma; *babhuuva* = became;
*paJNchaashadyojanocchritaH* = fifty yojanas high.

**अनुवादः**

Thereafter, Hanuma got angry and became thirty yojanas long. Surasa made her mouth
forty yojanas high. The courageous Hanuma then became fifty yojanas high.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Then Hanūmān, incensed, swelled to thirty _yojanas_ in breadth (_triṃśadyojanam āyataḥ_), and Surasā made her mouth forty _yojanas_ high (_catvāriṃśat tathocchritam_).
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**162 (Text-Critical Debate Over the Escalating Numbers)**

Both _Tilaka_ and Govindarāja discuss at length the authenticity of the incremental ten-by-ten expansion verses (from twenty up to a hundred _yojanas_). Govindarāja rejects the entire escalation sequence as an inconsistent interpolation (प्रक्षिप्ताः असङ्गताश्च), arguing that a hundred-_yojana_-wide Hanūmān would already touch Laṅkā (शतयोजनायतत्वे वानरैर्लङ्कावासिभिश्च दृष्टः स्यादिति विरोधात्), while _Tilaka_ similarly notes that older manuscripts jump directly from twenty _yojanas_ to Hanūmān shrinking to thumb-size (इति श्लोकास्तु प्रक्षिप्ता इति कतकः). _Śiromaṇi_, however, comments upon every step of the traditional Vulgate sequence.
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

As Surasā expands her jaws to a hundred yojanas, the sagacious Hanūmān shrinks to the size of a thumb, darts in and out of her mouth, and stands in the sky.
</details>
</details>

</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

यह देख वीर हनुमान् पचास योजन ऊँचे हो गये । तब सुरसाने अपना मुँह साठ योजन ऊँचा बना लिया ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आग वीरनाद हनुमन्तनु ऐवत्तु योजनगळष्टु विशालवागि तन्न शरीरवन्नु बॆळॆसिदनु. मरुक्षणदल्ले सुरसॆय बायि अरवत्तु योजनगळष्टु विस्तारवायितु.॥163॥
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

The heroic Hanūmān became fifty _yojanas_ high (_pañcāśadyojanocchritaḥ_), and Surasā made her mouth sixty _yojanas_ high (_ṣaṣṭiṃ yojanam ucchritam_).
</details>


<details><summary>टीका-notes (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**163–165 (The Climactic Escalation to a Hundred Yojanas)**

While Govindarāja and Rāma Varmā's _Tilaka_ regard the ten-by-ten escalation from thirty to ninety _yojanas_ as an interpolation lacking in older manuscripts, Śivasahāya's _Śiromaṇi_ glosses each stage in detail. On 5.1.164d and 165b, _Śiromaṇi_ specifically explains the bold epic syntax (बहिर्भूतत्वेऽप्यन्वय आर्षः) whereby the accusative numerals अशीतिम् and नवतिम् stand outside the compound योजनोच्छ्रित- (`eighty/ninety _yojanas_ high').
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तदैव हनुमान् वीरः सप्ततिं योजनोच्छ्रितः ।  
चकार सुरसा वक्त्रमशीतिं योजनोच्छ्रितम् ॥ १६४ ॥
</details>

चकार सुरसा वक्त्रं मशीतीयोजनायतम् ॥
</details>

</details>

</details>

**पदच्छेदः**

163;164. 163; *164. tataH* = thereafter; *hanumaaMstu* = Hanuma;
*kruddhaH* = became angry; *triMshadyojanam aayataH* = became thirty yojanas long;
*surasaa* = Surasa; *chakaara* = made; *vaktram* = mouth;
*chatvaariMshat* = forty yojanas; *ucchritam* = high; *viiraH* = the
courageous; *hanumaan* = Hanuma; *babhuuva* = became;
*paJNchaashadyojanocchritaH* = fifty yojanas high.

**अनुवादः**

Thereafter, Hanuma got angry and became thirty yojanas long. Surasa made her mouth
forty yojanas high. The courageous Hanuma then became fifty yojanas high.

At that very moment (_tadaiva_) the heroic Hanūmān became seventy _yojanas_ high (_saptatiṃ yojanocchritaḥ_), and Surasā made her mouth eighty _yojanas_ high (_aśītiṃ yojanocchritam_).
</details>
</details>

</details>

</details>

तथैव हनुमान्वीरः सप्ततीयोजिनोच्छ्रितः ॥ ५-१-१६५

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

तदनन्तर अग्निके समान तेजस्वी हनुमान् नब्बे योजन ऊँचे हो गये । यह देख सुरसाने भी अपने मुँहका विस्तार सौ योजनका कर लिया* ॥ १६५ ॥

\\* १६२ से लेकर १६५ तकके चार श्लोक कुछ टीकाकारोंने प्रक्षिप्त बताये हैं, किंतु रामायणशिरोमणि नामक टीकामें इनकी व्याख्या उपलब्ध होती है । अतः यहाँ मूलमें इन्हें सम्मिलित कर लिया गया है ।
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आग हनुमन्तन शरीरवु तॊम्भत्तु योजनगळष्टु दॊड्डदायितु. अदक्कॆ अनुगुणवागि सुरसॆय आस्यवु नूरु योजनगळष्टु विस्तारवायितु. ॥165 ॥
</details>

<details><summary>Murali Krishnamurthy Kopalle Murthy</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

her mouth; *shhashhTi yojanam aayatam* = sixty yojanas long; *viiraH* = the

Like blazing fire (_analaprakhyaḥ_), Hanūmān rose to ninety _yojanas_ in height (_navatiṃ yojanocchritaḥ_), and Surasā made her mouth a hundred _yojanas_ wide (_śatayojanam āyatam_).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तद् दृष्ट्वा व्यादितं त्वास्यं वायुपुत्रः स बुद्धिमान् ।  
दीर्घजिह्वं सुरसया सुभीमं नरकोपमम् ॥ १६६ ॥  
स सङ्क्षिप्यात्मनः कायं जीमूत इव मारुतिः ।  
तस्मिन् मुहूर्ते हनुमान् बभूवाङ्गुष्ठमात्रकः ॥ १६७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद् दृष्ट्वा व्यादितं त्वास्यं वायुपुत्रः स बुद्धिमान् ।  
दीर्घजिह्वं सुरसया सुभीमं नरकोपमम् ॥ १६६ ॥  
स सङ्क्षिप्यात्मनः कायं जीमूत इव मारुतिः ।  
तस्मिन् मुहूर्ते हनुमान् बभूवाङ्गुष्ठमात्रकः ॥ १६७ ॥
_________________
तद्दृष्ट्वा व्यादितं त्वास्यं वायुपुत्रः सुबुद्धिमान् ।  
दीर्घजिह्वं सुरसया सुभीमं नरकोपमम् ॥
स सङ्क्षिप्यात्मनः कायं जीमूत इव मारुतिः ।  
तस्मिन्मुहूर्ते हनूमान्बभूवाङ्गुष्ठ मात्रकः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

चकार सुरसा व्क्त्रंशीतीयोजनोच्छ्रितम् ।
हनुमानचलप्रख्यो नवतीयोजनोच्छ्रितः ॥ ५-१-१६६
